# Manual review queue (not on the map)

Built 23 Oct 2026. Nothing here is a pin. Ready items were checked against own sites or reliable listings; add only on Dean's OK.

## Verified, ready to add
- **One Roof Leicester Winter Night Shelter** (shelter): Every night 5 Oct 2026 – 31 Mar 2027. Referral: Referral only via Leicester City/County Outreach (alert StreetLink). Address: Leicester (venue not published). Phone: none confirmed. Source: https://www.oneroof.org.uk/our-work/winter-night-shelter. Venue undisclosed, so no exact pin: show as a city-level referral listing
- **Robes Winter Night Shelter (Southwark & Lambeth)** (shelter): 2026-27 season, 7 nights a week, Nov 2026 onward (Amott Road Thursdays Nov 2026 – mid Jan 2027). Referral: Referral only via partner day centres/outreach (Manna Centre, Spires, Ace of Clubs, Webber St, Crisis, The Passage, Southwark SPOT, Thames Reach Lambeth). Address: Rotating churches, Southwark & Lambeth. Phone: none confirmed. Source: https://robes.org.uk/get-help-now/ ; https://www.stewardship.org.uk/pages/Nightshelter2026. Phone not on own site (Pavement lists 020 7407 5623, not confirmed)
- **Glass Door Winter Night Shelters (3 circuits)** (shelter): 21 Nov 2026 – 22 Mar 2027. Referral: Open access via Glass Door referral/casework. Address: Rotating venues, Kensington & Chelsea, Hammersmith & Fulham, Wandsworth. Phone: none confirmed. Source: https://www.glassdoor.org.uk/blog/two-month-winter-night-shelter-update ; Glass Door 2026-27 job pack (charityjob). 2026-27 dates are from Glass Door's own job pack, not a public notice yet
- **Barnet Winter Night Shelter (St Mary-at-Finchley)** (shelter): Cycle one 8 Nov – 21 Dec 2026; St Mary's hosts Sunday nights, 22:00–06:30. Referral: Via Homeless Action in Barnet (HAB). Address: St Mary-at-Finchley, 26 Hendon Lane, London N3 1TR (Sundays only; rotating). Phone: 020 8446 8400 (HAB). Source: https://www.habcentre.org/winter-night-shelter/ ; https://stmaryatfinchley.org.uk/night-shelter-volunteers/. HAB phone came from search summary; re-check on habcentre.org before adding
- **Oxford Winter Night Shelter (OWNS)** (shelter): Every night Jan – end Mar 2027 (reopens Jan 2027). Referral: Referral via St Mungo's Outreach, The Gatehouse, The Porch or Crisis; must be rough sleeping. Address: Rotating church halls; office: The Living Room, 57c St Clements Street, Oxford OX4 1AG. Phone: 01865 362 799. Source: https://www.ownsoxford.org.uk/ ; https://oxfordshirehomelessmovement.org/organisation/oxford-winter-night-shelter-owns. Not open until Jan 2027: add with 'Opens Jan 2027'
- **Evergreen Care UK – Elderberries Vintage Tea Rooms (8th Bexleyheath Scout Group)** (warm space): Opened 1 Oct 2026; Thursdays 10:30–13:00. Referral: Drop-in (older people focus). Address: 8th Bexleyheath Scout Group, 32 Westbrooke Road, Welling DA16 1PR. Phone: none confirmed. Source: https://www.evergreencareuk.org/elderberries-tearooms ; Warm Welcome feed id 68015582. 
- **Signpost Tendring – The Kettle's On** (warm space): Opened 1 Oct 2026; Warm Welcome lists Tue 11:00–14:00; own site lists Mon–Fri 9:30–15:30. Referral: Drop-in, no appointment. Address: Unit 5, Jaywick Enterprise Centre, Loftus Way, Jaywick, Clacton-on-Sea CO15 2LU. Phone: 01255 688683. Source: https://www.sign-post.info/home/core-services/signpost-tendring ; Warm Welcome feed id 68015583. Use own-site hours
- **The Hub, South Brent (SBADC)** (warm space): Reopens 1 Nov 2026 after refurbishment; Mon–Fri 09:30–12:30. Referral: Drop-in. Address: South Brent Old School Community Centre, South Brent, Devon TQ10 9BP. Phone: 01364 700282. Source: https://www.sbadc.org.uk ; Warm Welcome feed id 67055044. Add with 'Opens 1 Nov 2026'

## Rejected (not confirmed or duplicate)
- **C4WS Homeless Project winter shelter (Camden)**: Genuine, recurring (early Nov – end Mar, agency referral only), but no 2026-27 dates published yet; re-check in Nov (https://c4wshomelessproject.org/i-am-homeless)
- **Arun Leisure Centre**: Address in feed is Littlehampton Wave (BN16 2NA), not Arun Leisure Centre; looks like a duplicate/mis-entry of the Littlehampton listing; not confirmed on own site (Warm Welcome feed id 69175072)
- **Bath Abbey (Cosy Place)**: Real, but 2026-27 run (6 Oct 2026 – 6 Apr 2027) only in the feed; council page shows 2025-26 and different hours (12:30–14:00 vs 13:30) (https://livewell.bathnes.gov.uk/cost-living-crisis/warm-places-list/bath-abbeys-cosy-place)
- **Littlehampton Wave**: Only 2025 listings found (Thu 9–1); feed says Thu 10–12 from 1 Dec 2026; not confirmed on own site (https://www.healthwatchwestsussex.co.uk/advice-and-information/2025-12-11/warm-spaces-arun)
- **Pineholt Bowls Club**: No website; nothing beyond the feed to confirm (Warm Welcome feed id 68762729)
- **RNA Gosport**: No website; friendship/dementia group, not confirmed beyond the feed (Warm Welcome feed id 68922460)
- **Shimmy School Community Hub and Multifaith Centre**: No website; not confirmed beyond the feed (Warm Welcome feed id 69082338)
- **St Bene't's Church, Cambridge**: Own site does not mention the Tuesday TAG warm welcome; feed also has an old 2024 duplicate listing (https://www.stbenetschurch.org/)
- **Support for new life (Harrow)**: No opening hours in listing (all days closed) and broken website link (Warm Welcome feed id 67876962)
- **The Drop In, St Nicholas Corfe Mullen**: Own site gave nothing readable; not confirmed (https://www.parishofcorfemullen.church)
